    LEFT JOIN [OnlineOrders] AS [o0] ON [o].[Id] = [o0].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [FromStoreOrders] AS [f] ON [o].[Id] = [f].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [UserAddress] AS [u] ON [o0].[ShippingAddressId] = [u].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [Store] AS [s] ON [f].[StoreId] = [s].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [Payment] AS [p] ON [o].[Id] = [p].[OrderId]
    LEFT JOIN [Clients] AS [c] ON [o].[ClientId] = [c].[Id]
    LEFT JOIN [AspNetUsers] AS [a] ON [c].[Id] = [a].[Id]
    WHERE [o].[Id] = @__orderId_0
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [o1].[Id], [o1].[InvetoryId], [o1].[OrderId], [o1].[ProductId], [o1].[Quantity], [o1].[ReservationId], [o1].[SubTotal], [o1].[UnitPrice], [p0].[ProductId] AS [ProductId0], [p0].[ActiveIngredients], [p0].[AverageRating], [p0].[CategoryId], [p0].[CompanyId], [p0].[Contraindications], [p0].[CreatedAt], [p0].[Description], [p0].[DiscountPercentage], [p0].[DosageForm], [p0].[IsAvailable], [p0].[IsDeleted], [p0].[MedicalDescription], [p0].[NameAr], [p0].[NameEn], [p0].[Price], [p0].[SideEffects], [p0].[Tags], [p0].[TotalRatings], [p0].[UpdatedAt], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[IsPrimary], [p1].[ProductId] AS [ProductId1], [p1].[Url]
    FROM [OrderItem] AS [o1]
    INNER JOIN [Products] AS [p0] ON [o1].[ProductId] = [p0].[ProductId]
    LEFT JOIN [ProductImage] AS [p1] ON [p0].[ProductId] = [p1].[ProductId]
) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id] = [t0].[OrderId]
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [t].[Id0], [t].[Id1], [t].[Id2], [t].[Id3], [t].[Id4], [t0].[Id], [t0].[ProductId0]
